### BauwerkEra (6)
1347 1900–1945 | beschreibung=Industrialisation, reinforced concrete commercialised, PAK still high, some early asbestos products.
1348 1970–1990 | beschreibung=Still high asbestos / PCB / KMF, energy crisis drives insulation upgrades, first heavy-metal restrictions.
1349 1990–2000 | beschreibung=Country-specific asbestos bans (DE 1993, NL 1994, FR 1997, BE 1998, UK 2000), KMF biopersistence rules tighten.
1350 nach 2000 | beschreibung=Modern. Asbestos banned across EU/EEA, tight formaldehyde emission limits.
1351 Nachkrieg 1945–1970 | beschreibung=Wiederaufbau / Wirtschaftswunder. Highest asbestos use, PCB sealants begin, mineral wool widely installed.
1352 vor 1900 | beschreibung=Pre-industrial / Wilhelmine. PAK from coal tar high, lime mortars, structural variance very high.
